[permission_mysql_8_0_00025]
source: lineage/mysql/complex.txt#MyParseColumnComplexTest.test1
sql:
-- 包含子查询的跨Schema三表连接[1,7](@ref)
SELECT s1t1.column1 AS main_id,
       (SELECT MAX(s2t2.column3)
        FROM schema2.table2 s2t2
        WHERE s2t2.column4 = s1t1.column2) AS max_val,
       RANK() OVER (ORDER BY s1t2.column5 DESC) AS rank_level
FROM schema1.table1 s1t1
JOIN schema1.table2 s1t2
  ON s1t1.column3 = s1t2.column1
LEFT JOIN schema2.table1 s2t1
  ON s1t1.column4 = s2t1.column5
WHERE s2t1.column6 IN (
    SELECT DISTINCT column2
    FROM schema2.table2
    WHERE column4 > 100
);
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1
expect:
{
  "READ": [
    "Table(4:13~4:27) /test/1/catalog1/schema2/table2/",
    "Table(7:5~7:19) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/table1/",
    "Table(8:5~8:19) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/table2/",
    "Table(10:10~10:24) /test/1/catalog1/schema2/table1/"
  ]
}
----------

[permission_mysql_8_0_00027]
source: lineage/mysql/complex.txt#MyParseColumnComplexTest.test2
sql:
SELECT s1t1.column2 AS category,
       SUM(s2t1.column4) AS total_sum,
       AVG(s1t2.column5) OVER (PARTITION BY s1t1.column2) AS avg_value,
       (SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM schema2.table2
        WHERE column3 = s1t1.column3) AS related_count
FROM schema1.table1 s1t1
LEFT JOIN schema1.table2 s1t2
  ON s1t1.column1 = s1t2.column6
RIGHT JOIN schema2.table1 s2t1
  ON s1t1.column4 = s2t1.column3
GROUP BY s1t1.column2
HAVING total_sum > (
    SELECT AVG(column4)*1.5
    FROM schema2.table1
);
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1
expect:
{
  "READ": [
    "Table(5:13~5:27) /test/1/catalog1/schema2/table2/",
    "Table(7:5~7:19) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/table1/",
    "Table(8:10~8:24) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/table2/",
    "Table(10:11~10:25) /test/1/catalog1/schema2/table1/"
  ]
}
----------
[permission_mysql_8_0_00029]
source: lineage/mysql/complex.txt#MyParseColumnComplexTest.test3
sql:
(
    SELECT
        s1t1.*,
        s2t2.column1 AS s2_col1,
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY s1t2.column5) AS conflict_alias
    FROM schema1.table1 s1t1
    LEFT JOIN schema2.table2 s2t2
      ON s1t1.column6 = s2t2.column3
    LEFT JOIN schema1.table2 s1t2
      ON s1t1.column2 = s1t2.column4
)
UNION
(
    SELECT
        s2t1.*,
        s1t2.column2 AS s1_col2,
        RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY s2t1.column3 ORDER BY s2t1.column4) AS conflict_alias
    FROM schema2.table1 s2t1
    RIGHT JOIN schema1.table2 s1t2
      ON s2t1.column5 = s1t2.column1
    WHERE s2t1.column6 IN (
        SELECT MAX(column6)
        FROM schema2.table2
        GROUP BY column2
    )
)
ORDER BY
    CASE
        WHEN conflict_alias > 100 THEN column1
        ELSE column2
    END DESC;
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1
expect:
{
  "READ": [
    "Table(6:9~6:23) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/table1/",
    "Table(7:14~7:28) /test/1/catalog1/schema2/table2/",
    "Table(9:14~9:28) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/table2/",
    "Table(18:9~18:23) /test/1/catalog1/schema2/table1/"
  ]
}
----------

CREATE PROCEDURE p1()
BEGIN
  DECLARE dbl DOUBLE;
  DECLARE deci DECIMAL(10, 2);
  DECLARE intv INTEGER;
  DECLARE str VARBINARY(16);
  SELECT DISTINCT VAR_POP(1) FROM proc_t1 GROUP BY pk INTO dbl;
  SELECT DISTINCT STDDEV_POP(1) FROM proc_t1 GROUP BY pk INTO dbl;
  SELECT DISTINCT STDDEV_SAMP(1) FROM proc_t1 GROUP BY pk INTO dbl;
  SELECT DISTINCT AVG(3.14159) FROM proc_t1 GROUP BY pk INTO deci;
  SELECT DISTINCT AVG(3.14159E0) FROM proc_t1 GROUP BY pk INTO dbl;
  SELECT DISTINCT BIT_OR(x'0000') FROM proc_t1 GROUP BY pk INTO str;
  SELECT DISTINCT BIT_OR(9) FROM proc_t1 GROUP BY pk INTO intv;
END;
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1

    "Table(7:34~7:41) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/proc_t1/"

CREATE PROCEDURE split_routine.proc_handler_sqlstate()
BEGIN
  DECLARE v_seen INT DEFAULT 0;
  DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR SQLSTATE '02000' SET v_seen = 1;
  SELECT id INTO @missing_id FROM split_routine.t WHERE id = -1;

[permission_mysql_8_0_00900]
source: split/mysql/5.6/ddl_mysql_stored_program_0.txt#3
sql:
CREATE PROCEDURE proc_compound_common(IN p_limit INT, OUT p_total INT)
COMMENT 'compound coverage'
LANGUAGE SQL
MODIFIES SQL DATA
SQL SECURITY INVOKER
BEGIN
  DECLARE v_id INT DEFAULT 0;
  DECLARE v_done BOOL DEFAULT FALSE;
  DECLARE cur_ids CURSOR FOR SELECT id FROM proc_src ORDER BY id;
  DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done = TRUE;
  SET p_total = 0;
  OPEN cur_ids;
  read_loop: LOOP
    FETCH NEXT FROM cur_ids INTO v_id;
    IF v_done THEN
      LEAVE read_loop;
    END IF;
    SET p_total = p_total + 1;
    CASE
      WHEN p_total < p_limit THEN SET v_id = v_id + 0;
      ELSE SET v_id = v_id;
    END CASE;
    IF p_total < p_limit THEN
      ITERATE read_loop;
    END IF;
  END LOOP read_loop;
  CLOSE cur_ids;
  WHILE p_total < p_limit DO
    SET p_total = p_total + 1;
  END WHILE;
  REPEAT
    SET p_total = p_total - 1;
  UNTIL p_total <= p_limit
  END REPEAT;
END;
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1
expect:
{
  "READ": [
    "Table(9:44~9:52) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/proc_src/"
  ]
}
----------
[permission_mysql_8_0_00910]
source: split/mysql/5.6/ddl_mysql_stored_program_0.txt#14
sql:
CREATE EVENT split_event.ev_compound
ON SCHEDULE EVERY (1 + 1) DAY
STARTS CURRENT_TIMESTAMP + INTERVAL 1 HOUR
ENDS CURRENT_TIMESTAMP + INTERVAL 7 DAY
ON COMPLETION PRESERVE
DISABLE
COMMENT 'compound event'
DO event_block: BEGIN
  DECLARE done BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE;
  DECLARE v_id INT;
  DECLARE cur CURSOR FOR SELECT id FROM split_event.event_log;
  DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET done = TRUE;
  OPEN cur;
  read_loop: LOOP
    FETCH cur INTO v_id;
    IF done THEN
      LEAVE read_loop;
    END IF;
    UPDATE split_event.event_log SET note = CONCAT('event-', v_id) WHERE id = v_id;
  END LOOP read_loop;
  CLOSE cur;
  CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM split_event.event_log WHERE id = 1) THEN
      INSERT INTO split_event.event_log VALUES (2, 'created') ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE note = VALUES(note);
    ELSE
      SET @event_case = 0;
  END CASE;
END event_block;
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1
expect:
{
  "READ": [
    "Table(11:40~11:61) /test/1/catalog1/split_event/event_log/",
    "ConfigKey(26:10~26:21) /test/1/event_case/"
  ]
}
----------

sql:
CREATE TRIGGER tr_bi_check_uniqueness_with_nulls
BEFORE INSERT ON t1 FOR EACH ROW
BEGIN
  IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM t1 WHERE t1.id <=> NEW.id AND t1.ts <=> NEW.ts) THEN
    SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Duplicated values not allowed.';
  END IF;
END;
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1
expect:
{
  "READ": [
    "Table(4:27~4:29) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/t1/"
  ]
}

CREATE PROCEDURE load_locale_format_table()
BEGIN
  DECLARE locale_list VARCHAR(1000) DEFAULT '
  es_AR,es_BO,es_CL,es_CO,es_CR,es_DO,es_EC,es_ES,es_GT,es_HN,
  es_MX,es_NI,es_PA,es_PE,es_PR,es_PY,es_SV,es_US,es_UY,es_VE';
  SET @fmt_stmt = 'INSERT INTO locale_format VALUES
                   (?, FORMAT(12131254123412541,2,?));';
  PREPARE stmt FROM @fmt_stmt;
  WHILE locale_list != '' DO
    SET @locale =
          TRIM(REPLACE((SUBSTRING_INDEX(locale_list, ',', 1)), '\n',''));
    EXECUTE stmt USING @locale, @locale;
    IF LOCATE(',', locale_list) > 0 THEN
      SET locale_list =
      SUBSTRING(locale_list, LOCATE(',', locale_list) + 1);
    ELSE
      SET locale_list = '';
    END IF;
  END WHILE;
  DEALLOCATE PREPARE stmt;
END;
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1
expect:
{
  "READ": [
    "ConfigKey(6:6~6:15) /test/1/fmt_stmt/",
    "ConfigKey(10:8~10:15) /test/1/locale/"
  ]
}

CREATE PROCEDURE split_select_short.p1()
BEGIN
  DECLARE counter INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
  DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR SQLEXCEPTION BEGIN SET counter=counter+1; END;
  REPEAT
    IF RAND()>0.5 THEN START TRANSACTION; END IF;
    IF RAND()>0.5 THEN SELECT COUNT(*) FROM split_select_short.t FOR UPDATE; END IF;
    UPDATE split_select_short.t SET a=1 WHERE a>=0;
    SET counter=counter+1;
  UNTIL counter>=2 END REPEAT;
END;
